## Found, not fixed

- PR10 accepted P2 documentation limitation: the socket query schema declares only v=3, as required by the spec; epoch/cursor/clock/projection are documented in the channel description. The generated input schema permits additional properties, the current browser explicitly sets all resume/capability parameters, and the server reads them directly with unchanged first-value semantics. No captured generated socket consumer loses them; the lead did not confirm the reported reconnect regression. Accept the machine-readable query-completeness limitation for this bundle, retaining current-client and linked-replay coverage. Downstream generators remain unvalidated.

2026-10-28 PR9b2 pagination observation: WebKit feed-threads on source85f9b255 displayed97 loaded cards but retained Load more after the final GET /feed response returned next:null and a bounded refresh followed. The captured responses replay correctly through the unchanged reducer/refresh helper, and three unchanged focused browser runs passed. Cause remains unproven; no pagination behavior change is included. Failed trace/log and full retry are recorded in verification history.

2026-10-27 PR9b2 discovery scheduling observation: a loop-source-refresh received while useAgentSources is busy only sets pending. When queuedChanges is nonempty, schedule subsequently selects a changes-only pass and skips discovery. Current5272c885 trace shows no discovery after the re-add signal43834ms; existing source scheduling code is unchanged by this bundle. This is separate from the proposed one-line Feed publication fix. The fixture now waits for the prior source removal to reach Your agents before requesting another topology, preserving its intended sequential setup; scheduler behavior itself is left unchanged.

2026-10-27 PR9b2 linked `GET /feed` observation: exact base6f72d775 and current1e8ad795 failed at the fresh progressive-unsave reload. Deterministic isolated WebSocket-reset reproduction now shows an empty local page cached after linked read cancellation; the global completed/cache guard then suppresses the first linked page while another source waits. A proposed one-line per-source cached-summary guard retains the whole-cache-eviction protection and passes the isolated forced regression in both browsers. It remains subject to full verification and explicit reviewer agreement under the PROMPT exception; it is not yet a merged fix. See the appended diagnosis and retained first/retry failure logs.

2026-10-27 PR9b2 observation for GET `/agent-sources`: the failing4655d3bd Feed fixture trace records metadata responses and another metadata pass but no discovery request after a refresh fired before the first metadata projection settled. The unchanged busy/pending/queuedChanges logic can coalesce that request into metadata work; attribution is a code-supported timing inference, not a demonstrated new contract regression. Main6f72d775 Chromium passes that scenario; its WebKit run fails a different later outage-recovery assertion. Preserve both traces/logs described in verification history. The fixture now waits for a visible source agent before asking for another topology, as it already does later in the same scenario. Production refresh scheduling is left unchanged under the bundle scope rule; no claim that the scheduling observation or separate baseline failure is fixed.

(behaviour defects exposed by typing, with route, evidence and why the fix is out of scope here)

## Decisions made during implementation

- PR 3b inventory: ten remaining core routes, matching the spec. Result updates require boolean `hidden`; the existing handler does not accept an empty partial body. Personal prompt attachments remain already-parsed opaque JSON, including older partial attachment objects; message/file metadata remains text. Preserve existing coercions, profile normalization, unknown-field stripping and file/redirect writers. No consumer exists for message search, personal prompts or the download redirect; other consumers migrate outside protected App.tsx.

- PR 1: Include the MCP, workflow and card-token routers omitted from the spec's file estimate, because otherwise exported raw routers would remain. Starting server list: 227 unique method/path entries; 14 existing typed contracts.
- PR 1: Preserve app route authorization stages with five private registrars named `app`, mounted at the original boundaries. Their `/api` prefix keeps request URLs unchanged for Better Auth and federation. Companion owner assignment remains before its auth routes.
- PR 1: Keep `projectionSnapshot` outside the contract wrapper through the registrar's `wrap` option, preserving synchronous body/watermark reads. Preserve Cards strict and case-sensitive routing and existing admission middleware.
- PR 1: Move typed client helpers beside private transport in `api.ts`, keeping `workspace-api.ts` exports compatible, so no exported string transport can bypass the named path union. Narrow source-routing types and existing string concatenations without changing serialized URLs.
- PR 1: Serialized file-download addresses are narrowed to the existing objects/artifact-file routes before authorized fetch. Their names are in the client legacy list; no response fields or server inputs change.
- PR 1: The production inventory test combines server and companion mounts, checking uniqueness within each profile and exact registry/list coverage across both; profile-exclusive routes cannot all be mounted in one app.

- PR 1: Full design exposed a pre-existing mail fixture mismatch: `readInWorkspace` already retries two 5xx reads, but the smoke injected only one 503 and expected an error. The fixture now keeps the source unavailable through automatic refreshes and retries, then restores it for the manual recovery/loading/navigation assertions. A trace showed an automatic refresh overlapping the manual read, so a fixed refusal count could not exhaust both reads. Production retry behavior is unchanged.

- PR 1: Rebased on notification categories PR #184 (`9e378158`); retained its preference schema and response fields while replacing only registration with the named registrar. No App.tsx or native-session change from that rebase.

- PR 1: Upstream #184 introduced the first migration outside the frozen numeric ledger. Two existing startup tests incorrectly compared all migrations to that historical ledger. They now verify every named completion and preservation of the historical numeric identities; the migration runner and SQL are unchanged.

- PR 1: The Feed-loading metadata-outage assertion used Playwright’s five-second default, shorter than the existing two- and four-second read retries. Its deadline is now ten seconds; cached data, outage visibility and explicit recovery assertions remain unchanged. Focused Chromium/WebKit checks pass.

- PR 1: Upstream #185 (`706240b2`) merged equivalent startup, mail and Feed-loading fixture fixes plus a draft-save race fix while this branch was validating. Rebased and retained all upstream versions, so those test/script fixes are no longer part of this PR’s diff. App.tsx still differs from the new base only at the 12 authorized string narrowings; reactive ledger and native-sessions.ts have no diff. All gates must run again on the new base.

- PR 1: Reactive 12a merged as #186 (`d41c67b6`); rebased cleanly after all gates passed on the previous base. Native-session imports continue to use its facade. No changes to native modules, the facade or the reactive ledger; no further App.tsx edits. Fresh gates are required on the new base.

- PR 2: User resolved the contradictory operationId requirements: use `cardList`, `cardChanges`, `cardDetail`, `cardTokens`, `cardTokenCreate`, `cardTokenRevoke` and document the operationId-only compatibility diff, including the four existing HEAD IDs. Parent generated from `8c6bb3d7` in `/tmp/contracts-02-parent`; all existing Cards operation content, component schemas and security schemes compare equal after those ten ID changes.
- PR 2: All three Cards reads currently accept both grants, with selection enforcement in the unchanged admission middleware. `card:results` records their minimum grant; generated `x-card-grants` retains both `results:read` and `cards:read`, alongside owner and paired-session alternatives. Token schemas move to a shared schema module to avoid a registry/admission import cycle; there is only one route registry.
- PR 2: Preserve lenient token-management queries and strict Cards collection/detail queries. The required NoBody contract now refuses JSON bodies on Cards GET/HEAD and token GET/HEAD/DELETE with 400; previous handlers ignored these bodies. This is the explicit input tightening for the fold. No response fields are dropped. Move the existing submit 202 status into its contract so the new workspace document describes its actual wire status.
- PR 2: Migrate token management, useCards and Feed to contract transports without changing source capture, cancellation, recovery or mutation replay. Keep the three protected App.tsx readers on a temporary contract-backed path adapter, as recorded above. A shared path builder serves both browser helpers and the integration client.
- PR 2: The generator registers request schemas separately from response schemas to use Zod's input and output modes correctly (notably transformed/defaulted attachment IDs). NoBody maps to an unsatisfiable JSON schema and no requestBody; unsupported other types still fail generation. Preserve published Cards component names and HEAD operations; new workspace contracts contribute their declared methods.
- PR 2: Do not update the reactive ledger's old Cards script references despite PR 2's instruction to do so: the user's explicit protection and process coordination rule take precedence. Only this bundle's verification guide and canonical docs/scripts are updated.

2026-10-25, PR 5 implementation decisions: migrate all 23 workspace registrations, including POST /uploads (omitted from the spec's enumerated list). Inspection of the actual handler disproves the spec's polymorphic-JSON description of GET /objects/:id: it sends stored bytes with attachment headers, or a sandboxed HTML preview for preview=html. Preserve that stream unchanged; there are no JSON kinds to union. Artifact content remains public and metadata/import-list summary remain opaque Text. Import preview retains its new/duplicate union and already-decoded producer summary; start retains active/new run versus already-completed receipts. No HTTP reparse of stored JSON.

PR 5 projection decisions: declare the two preference keys actually read by SearchSettings (embedding_enabled and embedding_connection), preserving the absent-row branch; drop owner_id and embedding_model. Drop search rank/vector retrieval internals, retaining all display/pagination fields and the shared string excerpt schema. Drop checkout capture_token, capture_started_at and capture_pending, internal coordination columns absent from its existing public TypeScript type. No browser caller reads these dropped fields. Search name/provider, import execution-only agentId/attachmentsStored and the absent-preference branch are current conditional shapes. Historical compatibility fields are search semantic/message_id (f28e61b2), checkout scope (52b679a2) and filtered (5679a467); production HTTP omission assertions and actual-response browser omission coverage are added. Existing PublicArtifact/PublicFolder are reused. GET NoBody rejects previously ignored JSON bodies; body-ignored mutations retain their lenience. Required upload text fields keep existing UUID validation; Multer limits and ownership checks remain.

PR 5 transport decisions: a multipart contract owns one file part and its text-field schema; per-route middleware runs before the typed handler so Express catches synchronous and asynchronous validation errors. The typed client retains source capture, abort signals, authorization and one-attempt mutations. Binary object links/previews now use routeUrl/fetchStream. Repeated query keys survive routePath serialization, preserving non-scalar preview behavior (and correcting PR 3b's first-preview-value limitation). Attachment timeout/size/image preparation and stale-source completion guards remain; other upload errors use the shared transport's messages. All unprotected workspace clients are migrated, with protected exceptions listed above. No SQL, run transitions, import cancellation or existing polling timer responsibilities changed.

PR8b compatibility decision resolving the comment concern: PR8 spec explicitly says all three GitHub write bodies carry a required head commit. contracts.md allows recorded input-tightening decisions; this specific requirement takes precedence over the generic no-behavior-change statement. Therefore the existing comment endpoint intentionally rejects the former {body}-only shape with Problem400 before dispatch; it does not synthesize a head, accept an unpinned fallback, or version into a second unpinned route. An older open tab must reload the updated client; an external caller must read detail and send headSha. This is a deliberate compatibility break, not a transparent migration. Evidence: PullCommentInput requires CommitSha; production route uses that schema; the sole production browser caller PullRequests.tsx:1341 sends its displayed pr.headSha; github-route-contracts.test.ts:267 explicitly tests the old shape returning400, and :334 checks moved-head409 with zero dispatch. The browser smoke verifies its supplied head and one refreshed action. PROMPT.md identifies the browser as the current consumer; repository search finds no other production caller. No claim that an older body remains valid. A newer browser remains compatible with the old server, which ignores this extra key. This decision is recorded in PR#215; no code changes, tests or assertions removed.
